53:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":"Build a merged template = bundled template + overlay files on top."}]
54:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Copies the bundled template into ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"staging_dir"}],", then copies the user’s\noverlay files over it (user wins, missing files fall back to the bundled\ntemplate). The overlay is scanned for symlinks first — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"shutil.copytree"}],"\ndereferences them, so an untrusted overlay could otherwise pull files from\noutside the tree into the published site. The build directories that are\nnever published are excluded from both the scan and the copy (so a symlink\nunder e.g. the overlay’s ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"content/"}]," is never copied either)."]}]
55:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":"The staging dir is recreated on every build, so two guards protect user\ndata: the overlay may not live inside the staging dir, and an existing\nstaging dir is only removed when it carries the Folio staging marker file\n(proving a previous Folio build created it)."}]
